        Idea:
Produce a design document that plans the full migration of reigh-worker's vendored deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP code from its current drifted snapshot (~50 days / ~9 minor versions behind, no .git directory) into a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, broken into 2-week sprints. The document's job is to define the sprints, not to execute them.

Non-negotiable constraint throughout every sprint: NO FUNCTIONALITY MAY BE LOST. Every behavior reigh-worker currently depends on — IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose/depth/canny/cameraman modes (source/core/params/travel_guidance.py _IC_LORA_BY_MODE, _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora), the newly-landed ltx_anchor travel guidance kind, the existing ltx_control and ltx_hybrid travel paths, the vendored ICLoraPipeline behavior, the custom patches our vendored tree carries — must survive, be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan, and have a verification step.

Context the document must incorporate (treat as authoritative background):

- Current situation: reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ is a flat copy without a .git directory. Cannot be rebased. Upstream is deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP. Approximately 50 days behind (vendored top changelog entry is v10.01 / 2026-01-01; upstream top is v10.951 / 2026-02-19). 22 files differ in Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ alone, ~4303 diff lines.
- Missing upstream features we want: ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json defaults, shared/utils/self_refiner.py (Feb 4), models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py (res_2s sampler), outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma preprocessing (_apply_gamma_to_media, LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA, LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO constants), prompt_enhancer improvements.
- Upstream's IC-LoRA pattern is simpler than ours: single union-control preload URL for distilled 22B (models/ltx2/ltx2.py:783-832), substring-matched, no per-mode Python dict like our _IC_LORA_BY_MODE. Upstream auto-forces guide_phases=1 when P/D/E in video_prompt_type (ltx2.py:918). Cameraman-style per-mode LoRAs are 19B (not 22B). This means a large chunk of our custom IC-LoRA registry logic is either convergeable to upstream's approach or should become a thin override.
- Detailer situation: LTX-2 detailer is 19B only (no 22B/2.3 variant exists). Lightricks' ICLoraPipeline runs LoRA stage-1-only with stage-2 latent chaining. Upstream has no LTX-2 detailer integration. self_refiner (PnP mid-denoise) is the nearest thing upstream offers and is likely to obviate inline detailing after we adopt it.
- User preference: detailer, if we build it, should be a post-gen task handler (like source/task_handlers/rife_interpolate.py) rather than inline in the travel pipeline. This is independent of the fork migration but is a natural follow-up sprint.
- Constraint inventory (what reigh-worker currently depends on that must survive):
  - IC-LoRA auto-injection by mode (source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987) wiring union-control to pose/depth/canny and cameraman LoRA to cameraman mode.
  - Pixel cross-fade guide video path for ltx_control (source/media/video/travel_guide.py) — must NOT be broken by the resync even though it's arguably legacy.
  - ltx_anchor kind just shipped in commit 9656cc90 — must survive the resync. The rename _build_segment_hybrid_guidance_config → _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config touched vendored tests indirectly; verify.
  - The vendored Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79 clear_conditioning that strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode — load-bearing for ltx_anchor, must not be accidentally reverted to an upstream version lacking it if we have patches here.
  - IC-LoRA dedup logic (task_registry.py:961-968).
  - Travel orchestrator PATH A skip for ltx_anchor (orchestrator.py:1278-1289) and _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config rename (orchestrator.py).
  - Existing test suite: tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py (20 ltx_anchor tests), tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py (8 runtime tests incl. source tripwire), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py (23 tests, recently promoted to tracked via .gitignore allowlist), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py, tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py, tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py.

Open design decisions the document must resolve (as Settled Decisions with rationale):

1. Fork shape: submodule pinned to upstream SHA vs. our own long-running fork on GitHub (e.g. banodoco/Wan2GP) vs. monkeypatch-only with upstream as a dep. Probably a hybrid — fork + minimal monkeypatch layer for things that shouldn't be in the fork. Document must pick one concretely.
2. Patch triage buckets: every deviation between our vendored tree and upstream gets classified as (a) accept upstream — stale not intentional, (b) upstreamable — PR to deepbeepmeep, (c) banodoco-specific — carry in fork or as override, (d) cruft — drop. Document must define the triage process as a sprint task.
3. Initial resync cadence: do we cut over to upstream head all at once, or stage by subsystem (e.g., models/ltx2/ first, then shared/, then postprocessing/)? Document must pick.
4. LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion: bundle into the migration, or hold for a separate A/B sprint? Document must pick.
5. res_2s / self_refiner exposure: off-by-default flags, or promote to default once upstream does? Document must pick.
6. Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion: does this sit inside the fork migration sprint plan (as a sprint 3 or 4), or is it a separate initiative? Document must pick.
7. Rebase cadence post-migration: weekly? Monthly? Triggered by specific upstream events? Document must pick.
8. CI / drift detection: how do we avoid re-drifting once we're synced? Document must propose a specific mechanism (scheduled CI job comparing to upstream head, etc.).

Target shape of the final document:
- Executive summary (what, why, how long, what we're protecting)
- Current-state inventory (what's vendored, what's drifted, what's load-bearing)
- Fork architecture decision (the Settled Decisions on shape, patch triage, cadence)
- Sprint breakdown: 3-5 sprints of 2 weeks each, each with: goals, deliverables, verification gates, rollback plan, explicit functionality-preservation checks. First sprint should be inventory-heavy (the diff triage is where the risk lives). Last sprint should cover sustainability (rebase cadence, CI, detection).
- Risk register: what could go wrong per sprint, probability, impact, mitigation
- Success criteria: objective checks for migration complete vs migration in progress vs regression observed
- Explicit Settled Decisions section using the canonical megaplan format (id: SD-NNN, load_bearing, decision, rationale)

Out of scope (document should note these explicitly as non-goals): flipping ltx_anchor to default, real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults, upscaler-pass anchor re-injection, identity-drift fixes.

Honor the memory-note constraint: do NOT include hard-halt prerequisite clauses of the form halt when MX not shipped. Each sprint must be able to proceed, revise scope, or explicitly deprioritize — but not sit in force-proceed limbo.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: the attached success criteria are stale and do not match the current plan body. they still require 'no `usestate` for `activepairdata`', '`setactivepairdata` fully removed', 'sync effect deleted', and '`onpairclick` simplified to `(pairindex: number) => void`', which are the opposite of the current targeted-sync plan. as presented, the criteria are not usable for review or execution. > DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker

        Idea:
Produce a design document that plans the full migration of reigh-worker's vendored deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP code from its current drifted snapshot (~50 days / ~9 minor versions behind, no .git directory) into a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, broken into 2-week sprints. The document's job is to define the sprints, not to execute them.

Non-negotiable constraint throughout every sprint: NO FUNCTIONALITY MAY BE LOST. Every behavior reigh-worker currently depends on — IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose/depth/canny/cameraman modes (source/core/params/travel_guidance.py _IC_LORA_BY_MODE, _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora), the newly-landed ltx_anchor travel guidance kind, the existing ltx_control and ltx_hybrid travel paths, the vendored ICLoraPipeline behavior, the custom patches our vendored tree carries — must survive, be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan, and have a verification step.

Context the document must incorporate (treat as authoritative background):

- Current situation: reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ is a flat copy without a .git directory. Cannot be rebased. Upstream is deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP. Approximately 50 days behind (vendored top changelog entry is v10.01 / 2026-01-01; upstream top is v10.951 / 2026-02-19). 22 files differ in Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ alone, ~4303 diff lines.
- Missing upstream features we want: ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json defaults, shared/utils/self_refiner.py (Feb 4), models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py (res_2s sampler), outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma preprocessing (_apply_gamma_to_media, LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA, LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO constants), prompt_enhancer improvements.
- Upstream's IC-LoRA pattern is simpler than ours: single union-control preload URL for distilled 22B (models/ltx2/ltx2.py:783-832), substring-matched, no per-mode Python dict like our _IC_LORA_BY_MODE. Upstream auto-forces guide_phases=1 when P/D/E in video_prompt_type (ltx2.py:918). Cameraman-style per-mode LoRAs are 19B (not 22B). This means a large chunk of our custom IC-LoRA registry logic is either convergeable to upstream's approach or should become a thin override.
- Detailer situation: LTX-2 detailer is 19B only (no 22B/2.3 variant exists). Lightricks' ICLoraPipeline runs LoRA stage-1-only with stage-2 latent chaining. Upstream has no LTX-2 detailer integration. self_refiner (PnP mid-denoise) is the nearest thing upstream offers and is likely to obviate inline detailing after we adopt it.
- User preference: detailer, if we build it, should be a post-gen task handler (like source/task_handlers/rife_interpolate.py) rather than inline in the travel pipeline. This is independent of the fork migration but is a natural follow-up sprint.
- Constraint inventory (what reigh-worker currently depends on that must survive):
  - IC-LoRA auto-injection by mode (source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987) wiring union-control to pose/depth/canny and cameraman LoRA to cameraman mode.
  - Pixel cross-fade guide video path for ltx_control (source/media/video/travel_guide.py) — must NOT be broken by the resync even though it's arguably legacy.
  - ltx_anchor kind just shipped in commit 9656cc90 — must survive the resync. The rename _build_segment_hybrid_guidance_config → _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config touched vendored tests indirectly; verify.
  - The vendored Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79 clear_conditioning that strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode — load-bearing for ltx_anchor, must not be accidentally reverted to an upstream version lacking it if we have patches here.
  - IC-LoRA dedup logic (task_registry.py:961-968).
  - Travel orchestrator PATH A skip for ltx_anchor (orchestrator.py:1278-1289) and _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config rename (orchestrator.py).
  - Existing test suite: tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py (20 ltx_anchor tests), tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py (8 runtime tests incl. source tripwire), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py (23 tests, recently promoted to tracked via .gitignore allowlist), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py, tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py, tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py.

Open design decisions the document must resolve (as Settled Decisions with rationale):

1. Fork shape: submodule pinned to upstream SHA vs. our own long-running fork on GitHub (e.g. banodoco/Wan2GP) vs. monkeypatch-only with upstream as a dep. Probably a hybrid — fork + minimal monkeypatch layer for things that shouldn't be in the fork. Document must pick one concretely.
2. Patch triage buckets: every deviation between our vendored tree and upstream gets classified as (a) accept upstream — stale not intentional, (b) upstreamable — PR to deepbeepmeep, (c) banodoco-specific — carry in fork or as override, (d) cruft — drop. Document must define the triage process as a sprint task.
3. Initial resync cadence: do we cut over to upstream head all at once, or stage by subsystem (e.g., models/ltx2/ first, then shared/, then postprocessing/)? Document must pick.
4. LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion: bundle into the migration, or hold for a separate A/B sprint? Document must pick.
5. res_2s / self_refiner exposure: off-by-default flags, or promote to default once upstream does? Document must pick.
6. Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion: does this sit inside the fork migration sprint plan (as a sprint 3 or 4), or is it a separate initiative? Document must pick.
7. Rebase cadence post-migration: weekly? Monthly? Triggered by specific upstream events? Document must pick.
8. CI / drift detection: how do we avoid re-drifting once we're synced? Document must propose a specific mechanism (scheduled CI job comparing to upstream head, etc.).

Target shape of the final document:
- Executive summary (what, why, how long, what we're protecting)
- Current-state inventory (what's vendored, what's drifted, what's load-bearing)
- Fork architecture decision (the Settled Decisions on shape, patch triage, cadence)
- Sprint breakdown: 3-5 sprints of 2 weeks each, each with: goals, deliverables, verification gates, rollback plan, explicit functionality-preservation checks. First sprint should be inventory-heavy (the diff triage is where the risk lives). Last sprint should cover sustainability (rebase cadence, CI, detection).
- Risk register: what could go wrong per sprint, probability, impact, mitigation
- Success criteria: objective checks for migration complete vs migration in progress vs regression observed
- Explicit Settled Decisions section using the canonical megaplan format (id: SD-NNN, load_bearing, decision, rationale)

Out of scope (document should note these explicitly as non-goals): flipping ltx_anchor to default, real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults, upscaler-pass anchor re-injection, identity-drift fixes.

Honor the memory-note constraint: do NOT include hard-halt prerequisite clauses of the form halt when MX not shipped. Each sprint must be able to proceed, revise scope, or explicitly deprioritize — but not sit in force-proceed limbo.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: the attached success criteria are stale and do not match the current plan body. they still require 'no `usestate` for `activepairdata`', '`setactivepairdata` fully removed', 'sync effect deleted', and '`onpairclick` simplified to `(pairindex: number) => void`', which are the opposite of the current targeted-sync plan. as presented, the criteria are not usable for review or execution. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker

        Idea:
Produce a design document that plans the full migration of reigh-worker's vendored deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP code from its current drifted snapshot (~50 days / ~9 minor versions behind, no .git directory) into a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, broken into 2-week sprints. The document's job is to define the sprints, not to execute them.

Non-negotiable constraint throughout every sprint: NO FUNCTIONALITY MAY BE LOST. Every behavior reigh-worker currently depends on — IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose/depth/canny/cameraman modes (source/core/params/travel_guidance.py _IC_LORA_BY_MODE, _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora), the newly-landed ltx_anchor travel guidance kind, the existing ltx_control and ltx_hybrid travel paths, the vendored ICLoraPipeline behavior, the custom patches our vendored tree carries — must survive, be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan, and have a verification step.

Context the document must incorporate (treat as authoritative background):

- Current situation: reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ is a flat copy without a .git directory. Cannot be rebased. Upstream is deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP. Approximately 50 days behind (vendored top changelog entry is v10.01 / 2026-01-01; upstream top is v10.951 / 2026-02-19). 22 files differ in Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ alone, ~4303 diff lines.
- Missing upstream features we want: ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json defaults, shared/utils/self_refiner.py (Feb 4), models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py (res_2s sampler), outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma preprocessing (_apply_gamma_to_media, LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA, LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO constants), prompt_enhancer improvements.
- Upstream's IC-LoRA pattern is simpler than ours: single union-control preload URL for distilled 22B (models/ltx2/ltx2.py:783-832), substring-matched, no per-mode Python dict like our _IC_LORA_BY_MODE. Upstream auto-forces guide_phases=1 when P/D/E in video_prompt_type (ltx2.py:918). Cameraman-style per-mode LoRAs are 19B (not 22B). This means a large chunk of our custom IC-LoRA registry logic is either convergeable to upstream's approach or should become a thin override.
- Detailer situation: LTX-2 detailer is 19B only (no 22B/2.3 variant exists). Lightricks' ICLoraPipeline runs LoRA stage-1-only with stage-2 latent chaining. Upstream has no LTX-2 detailer integration. self_refiner (PnP mid-denoise) is the nearest thing upstream offers and is likely to obviate inline detailing after we adopt it.
- User preference: detailer, if we build it, should be a post-gen task handler (like source/task_handlers/rife_interpolate.py) rather than inline in the travel pipeline. This is independent of the fork migration but is a natural follow-up sprint.
- Constraint inventory (what reigh-worker currently depends on that must survive):
  - IC-LoRA auto-injection by mode (source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987) wiring union-control to pose/depth/canny and cameraman LoRA to cameraman mode.
  - Pixel cross-fade guide video path for ltx_control (source/media/video/travel_guide.py) — must NOT be broken by the resync even though it's arguably legacy.
  - ltx_anchor kind just shipped in commit 9656cc90 — must survive the resync. The rename _build_segment_hybrid_guidance_config → _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config touched vendored tests indirectly; verify.
  - The vendored Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79 clear_conditioning that strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode — load-bearing for ltx_anchor, must not be accidentally reverted to an upstream version lacking it if we have patches here.
  - IC-LoRA dedup logic (task_registry.py:961-968).
  - Travel orchestrator PATH A skip for ltx_anchor (orchestrator.py:1278-1289) and _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config rename (orchestrator.py).
  - Existing test suite: tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py (20 ltx_anchor tests), tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py (8 runtime tests incl. source tripwire), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py (23 tests, recently promoted to tracked via .gitignore allowlist), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py, tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py, tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py.

Open design decisions the document must resolve (as Settled Decisions with rationale):

1. Fork shape: submodule pinned to upstream SHA vs. our own long-running fork on GitHub (e.g. banodoco/Wan2GP) vs. monkeypatch-only with upstream as a dep. Probably a hybrid — fork + minimal monkeypatch layer for things that shouldn't be in the fork. Document must pick one concretely.
2. Patch triage buckets: every deviation between our vendored tree and upstream gets classified as (a) accept upstream — stale not intentional, (b) upstreamable — PR to deepbeepmeep, (c) banodoco-specific — carry in fork or as override, (d) cruft — drop. Document must define the triage process as a sprint task.
3. Initial resync cadence: do we cut over to upstream head all at once, or stage by subsystem (e.g., models/ltx2/ first, then shared/, then postprocessing/)? Document must pick.
4. LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion: bundle into the migration, or hold for a separate A/B sprint? Document must pick.
5. res_2s / self_refiner exposure: off-by-default flags, or promote to default once upstream does? Document must pick.
6. Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion: does this sit inside the fork migration sprint plan (as a sprint 3 or 4), or is it a separate initiative? Document must pick.
7. Rebase cadence post-migration: weekly? Monthly? Triggered by specific upstream events? Document must pick.
8. CI / drift detection: how do we avoid re-drifting once we're synced? Document must propose a specific mechanism (scheduled CI job comparing to upstream head, etc.).

Target shape of the final document:
- Executive summary (what, why, how long, what we're protecting)
- Current-state inventory (what's vendored, what's drifted, what's load-bearing)
- Fork architecture decision (the Settled Decisions on shape, patch triage, cadence)
- Sprint breakdown: 3-5 sprints of 2 weeks each, each with: goals, deliverables, verification gates, rollback plan, explicit functionality-preservation checks. First sprint should be inventory-heavy (the diff triage is where the risk lives). Last sprint should cover sustainability (rebase cadence, CI, detection).
- Risk register: what could go wrong per sprint, probability, impact, mitigation
- Success criteria: objective checks for migration complete vs migration in progress vs regression observed
- Explicit Settled Decisions section using the canonical megaplan format (id: SD-NNN, load_bearing, decision, rationale)

Out of scope (document should note these explicitly as non-goals): flipping ltx_anchor to default, real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults, upscaler-pass anchor re-injection, identity-drift fixes.

Honor the memory-note constraint: do NOT include hard-halt prerequisite clauses of the form halt when MX not shipped. Each sprint must be able to proceed, revise scope, or explicitly deprioritize — but not sit in force-proceed limbo.

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: the attached success criteria are stale and do not match the current plan body. they still require 'no `usestate` for `activepairdata`', '`setactivepairdata` fully removed', 'sync effect deleted', and '`onpairclick` simplified to `(pairindex: number) => void`', which are the opposite of the current targeted-sync plan. as presented, the criteria are not usable for review or execution. Produce a design document that plans the full migration of reigh-worker's vendored deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP code from its current drifted snapshot (~50 days / ~9 minor versions behind, no .git directory) into a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer relationship, broken into 2-week sprints. The document's job is to define the sprints, not to execute them.

Non-negotiable constraint throughout every sprint: NO FUNCTIONALITY MAY BE LOST. Every behavior reigh-worker currently depends on — IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose/depth/canny/cameraman modes (source/core/params/travel_guidance.py _IC_LORA_BY_MODE, _auto_inject_travel_guidance_lora), the newly-landed ltx_anchor travel guidance kind, the existing ltx_control and ltx_hybrid travel paths, the vendored ICLoraPipeline behavior, the custom patches our vendored tree carries — must survive, be explicitly accounted for in the sprint plan, and have a verification step.

Context the document must incorporate (treat as authoritative background):

- Current situation: reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ is a flat copy without a .git directory. Cannot be rebased. Upstream is deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP. Approximately 50 days behind (vendored top changelog entry is v10.01 / 2026-01-01; upstream top is v10.951 / 2026-02-19). 22 files differ in Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ alone, ~4303 diff lines.
- Missing upstream features we want: ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json defaults, shared/utils/self_refiner.py (Feb 4), models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py (res_2s sampler), outpaint IC-LoRA + gamma preprocessing (_apply_gamma_to_media, LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA, LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO constants), prompt_enhancer improvements.
- Upstream's IC-LoRA pattern is simpler than ours: single union-control preload URL for distilled 22B (models/ltx2/ltx2.py:783-832), substring-matched, no per-mode Python dict like our _IC_LORA_BY_MODE. Upstream auto-forces guide_phases=1 when P/D/E in video_prompt_type (ltx2.py:918). Cameraman-style per-mode LoRAs are 19B (not 22B). This means a large chunk of our custom IC-LoRA registry logic is either convergeable to upstream's approach or should become a thin override.
- Detailer situation: LTX-2 detailer is 19B only (no 22B/2.3 variant exists). Lightricks' ICLoraPipeline runs LoRA stage-1-only with stage-2 latent chaining. Upstream has no LTX-2 detailer integration. self_refiner (PnP mid-denoise) is the nearest thing upstream offers and is likely to obviate inline detailing after we adopt it.
- User preference: detailer, if we build it, should be a post-gen task handler (like source/task_handlers/rife_interpolate.py) rather than inline in the travel pipeline. This is independent of the fork migration but is a natural follow-up sprint.
- Constraint inventory (what reigh-worker currently depends on that must survive):
  - IC-LoRA auto-injection by mode (source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:955-987) wiring union-control to pose/depth/canny and cameraman LoRA to cameraman mode.
  - Pixel cross-fade guide video path for ltx_control (source/media/video/travel_guide.py) — must NOT be broken by the resync even though it's arguably legacy.
  - ltx_anchor kind just shipped in commit 9656cc90 — must survive the resync. The rename _build_segment_hybrid_guidance_config → _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config touched vendored tests indirectly; verify.
  - The vendored Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79 clear_conditioning that strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode — load-bearing for ltx_anchor, must not be accidentally reverted to an upstream version lacking it if we have patches here.
  - IC-LoRA dedup logic (task_registry.py:961-968).
  - Travel orchestrator PATH A skip for ltx_anchor (orchestrator.py:1278-1289) and _build_segment_anchor_guidance_config rename (orchestrator.py).
  - Existing test suite: tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py (20 ltx_anchor tests), tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py (8 runtime tests incl. source tripwire), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py (23 tests, recently promoted to tracked via .gitignore allowlist), tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py, tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py, tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py.

Open design decisions the document must resolve (as Settled Decisions with rationale):

1. Fork shape: submodule pinned to upstream SHA vs. our own long-running fork on GitHub (e.g. banodoco/Wan2GP) vs. monkeypatch-only with upstream as a dep. Probably a hybrid — fork + minimal monkeypatch layer for things that shouldn't be in the fork. Document must pick one concretely.
2. Patch triage buckets: every deviation between our vendored tree and upstream gets classified as (a) accept upstream — stale not intentional, (b) upstreamable — PR to deepbeepmeep, (c) banodoco-specific — carry in fork or as override, (d) cruft — drop. Document must define the triage process as a sprint task.
3. Initial resync cadence: do we cut over to upstream head all at once, or stage by subsystem (e.g., models/ltx2/ first, then shared/, then postprocessing/)? Document must pick.
4. LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion: bundle into the migration, or hold for a separate A/B sprint? Document must pick.
5. res_2s / self_refiner exposure: off-by-default flags, or promote to default once upstream does? Document must pick.
6. Post-gen detailer sprint inclusion: does this sit inside the fork migration sprint plan (as a sprint 3 or 4), or is it a separate initiative? Document must pick.
7. Rebase cadence post-migration: weekly? Monthly? Triggered by specific upstream events? Document must pick.
8. CI / drift detection: how do we avoid re-drifting once we're synced? Document must propose a specific mechanism (scheduled CI job comparing to upstream head, etc.).

Target shape of the final document:
- Executive summary (what, why, how long, what we're protecting)
- Current-state inventory (what's vendored, what's drifted, what's load-bearing)
- Fork architecture decision (the Settled Decisions on shape, patch triage, cadence)
- Sprint breakdown: 3-5 sprints of 2 weeks each, each with: goals, deliverables, verification gates, rollback plan, explicit functionality-preservation checks. First sprint should be inventory-heavy (the diff triage is where the risk lives). Last sprint should cover sustainability (rebase cadence, CI, detection).
- Risk register: what could go wrong per sprint, probability, impact, mitigation
- Success criteria: objective checks for migration complete vs migration in progress vs regression observed
- Explicit Settled Decisions section using the canonical megaplan format (id: SD-NNN, load_bearing, decision, rationale)

Out of scope (document should note these explicitly as non-goals): flipping ltx_anchor to default, real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults, upscaler-pass anchor re-injection, identity-drift fixes.

Honor the memory-note constraint: do NOT include hard-halt prerequisite clauses of the form halt when MX not shipped. Each sprint must be able to proceed, revise scope, or explicitly deprioritize — but not sit in force-proceed limbo.

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.
